data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/App.tsx","content":"import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';\nimport { PeriodFilter, KpiSummary } from './types';\nimport {\n  monthlySalesData,\n  filterDataByPeriod,\n  formatCurrency,\n  formatCount,\n} from './mockData';\nimport KpiCard from './components/KpiCard';\nimport ChartSection from './components/ChartSection';\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [period, setPeriod] = useState<PeriodFilter>('all');\n  const [activeMetric, setActiveMetric] = useState<\n    'all' | 'revenue' | 'customers' | 'averageSpend'\n  >('all');\n\n  const filteredData = useMemo(() => {\n    return filterDataByPeriod(monthlySalesData, period);\n  }, [period]);\n\n  const kpiSummaries = useMemo<KpiSummary[]>(() => {\n    if (filteredData.length === 0) return [];\n\n    const currentMonth = filteredData[filteredData.length - 1];\n    const prevMonth =\n      filteredData.length > 1\n        ? filteredData[filteredData.length - 2]\n        : currentMonth;\n\n    const calcDiff = (curr: number, prev: number) => {\n      if (prev === 0) return { diff: 0, isIncrease: true };\n      const change = ((curr - prev) / prev) * 100;\n      return {\n        diff: Math.abs(change),\n        isIncrease: change >= 0,\n      };\n    };\n\n    const revDiff = calcDiff(currentMonth.revenue, prevMonth.revenue);\n    const custDiff = calcDiff(currentMonth.customers, prevMonth.customers);\n    const spendDiff = calcDiff(currentMonth.averageSpend, prevMonth.averageSpend);\n\n    return [\n      {\n        title: '直近月 売上',\n        value: `¥${formatCurrency(currentMonth.revenue)}`,\n        diff: revDiff.diff,\n        isIncrease: revDiff.isIncrease,\n        unit: '円',\n      },\n      {\n        title: '直近月 客数',\n        value: formatCount(currentMonth.customers),\n        diff: custDiff.diff,\n        isIncrease: custDiff.isIncrease,\n        unit: '人',\n      },\n      {\n        title: '直近月 平均客単価',\n        value: `¥${formatCurrency(currentMonth.averageSpend)}`,\n        diff: spendDiff.diff,\n        isIncrease: spendDiff.isIncrease,\n        unit: '円',\n      },\n    ];\n  }, [filteredData]);\n\n  const totals = useMemo(() => {\n    const totalRev = filteredData.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + cur.revenue, 0);\n    const totalCust = filteredData.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + cur.customers, 0);\n    const avgSpend = totalCust > 0 ? Math.round(totalRev / totalCust) : 0;\n    return {\n      revenue: totalRev,\n      customers: totalCust,\n      averageSpend: avgSpend,\n    };\n  }, [filteredData]);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"dashboard-container\">\n      {/* ヘッダーエリア */}\n      <header className=\"dashboard-header\">\n        <div className=\"header-title-group\">\n          <h1 className=\"dashboard-title\">売上分析ダッシュボード</h1>\n          <p className=\"dashboard-subtitle\">\n            月次売上・客数・客単価の統合パフォーマンス管理\n          </p>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"filter-group\">\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className={`filter-btn ${period === 'all' ? 'filter-btn-active' : ''}`}\n            onClick={() => setPeriod('all')}\n          >\n            全期間（12ヶ月）\n          </button>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className={`filter-btn ${period === '6m' ? 'filter-btn-active' : ''}`}\n            onClick={() => setPeriod('6m')}\n          >\n            直近6ヶ月\n          </button>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className={`filter-btn ${period === '3m' ? 'filter-btn-active' : ''}`}\n            onClick={() => setPeriod('3m')}\n          >\n            直近3ヶ月\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </header>\n\n      {/* サマリーバナー */}\n      <div className=\"summary-banner\">\n        <div>\n          <span style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', opacity: 0.85 }}>選択期間 合計売上</span>\n          <div style={{ fontSize: '1.5rem', fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: '-0.02em' }}>\n            ¥{formatCurrency(totals.revenue)}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div>\n          <span style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', opacity: 0.85 }}>延べ来客数</span>\n          <div style={{ fontSize: '1.5rem', fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: '-0.02em' }}>\n            {formatCount(totals.customers)} 人\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div>\n          <span style={{ fontSize: '0.875rem', opacity: 0.85 }}>期間通算 客単価</span>\n          <div style={{ fontSize: '1.5rem', fontWeight: 700, letterSpacing: '-0.02em' }}>\n data={filteredData}\n          activeMetric={activeMetric}\n          onMetricChange={setActiveMetric}\n        />\n      </div>\n\n      {/* 詳細データテーブル */}\n      <div className=\"table-container\">\n        <table className=\"data-table\">\n          <thead>\n            <tr>\n              <th className=\"text-center\">月度</th>\n              <th className=\"text-right\">売上高</th>\n              <th className=\"text-right\">客数</th>\n              <th className=\"text-right\">平均客単価</th>\n              <th className=\"text-right\">前月比（売上）</th>\n            </tr>\n          </thead>\n          <tbody>\n            {filteredData.map((row, index) => {\n              const prev = index > 0 ? filteredData[index - 1] : null;\n              let diffText = '-';\n              let isPos = true;\n\n `${isPos ?               orientation=\"left\"\n                stroke={CHART_COLORS.customers}\n                tick={{ fill: CHART_COLORS.tick, fontSize: 12 }}\n                tickFormatter={formatYAxisCustomers}\n                tickLine={false}\n                axisLine={false}\n              />\n            )}\n\n            {activeMetric === 'averageSpend' && (\n              <YAxis\n                yAxisId=\"spend\"\n                orientation=\"left\"\n                stroke={CHART_COLORS.averageSpend}\n                tick={{ fill: CHART_COLORS.tick, fontSize: 12 }}\n                tickFormatter={formatYAxisSpend}\n                tickLine={false}\n                axisLine={false}\n                domain={['auto', 'auto']}\n              />\n            )}\n\n            {/* 右Y軸: 全体推移表示時の客数 */}\n            {activeMetric === 'all' && (\n              <YAxis\n                yAxisId=\"customers\"\n                orientation=\"right\"\n                stroke={CHART_COLORS.customers}\n                tick={{ fill: CHART_COLORS.tick, fontSize: 12 }}\n                tickFormatter={formatYAxisCustomers}\n                tickLine={false}\n                axisLine={false}\n              />\n            )}\n\n            <Tooltip\n              contentStyle={{\n                backgroundColor: CHART_COLORS.cardBg,\n                border: `1px solid ${CHART_COLORS.border}`,\n                borderRadius: '8px',\n                boxShadow: '0 4px 6px -1px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.08)',\n                fontSize: '12px',\n              }}\n              formatter={(value?: number | string | null, name?: string) => {\n                const numericValue = typeof value === 'number' ? value : 0;\n                if (!value && value !== 0) {\n                  return ['-', name ?? ''];\n                }\n                if (name === '売上') return [`¥${formatCurrency(numericValue)}`, name];\n                if (name === '客数') return [`${formatCount(numericValue)}人`, name];\n                if (name === '客単価') return [`¥${formatCurrency(numericValue)}`, name];\n                return [value, name ?? ''];\n              }}\n              labelStyle={{ fontWeight: 600, color: CHART_COLORS.title, marginBottom: '4px' }}\n            />\n            <Legend\n              wrapperStyle={{ paddingTop: '16px', fontSize: '13px' }}\n            />\n\n            {/* 売上棒グラフ */}\n            {(activeMetric === 'all' || activeMetric === 'revenue') && (\n              <Bar\n                yAxisId=\"revenue\"\n                dataKey=\"revenue\"\n                name=\"売上\"\n                fill={CHART_COLORS.revenue}\n                radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}\n                maxBarSize={48}\n              />\n            )}\n\n            {/* 客数グラフ: all時は折れ線、単体時は棒 */}\n            {activeMetric === 'all' && (\n              <Line\n                yAxisId=\"customers\"\n                type=\"monotone\"\n                dataKey=\"customers\"\n                name=\"客数\"\n                stroke={CHART_COLORS.customers}\n                strokeWidth={3}\n                dot={{ r: 4, fill: CHART_COLORS.customers, strokeWidth: 2, stroke: '#ffffff' }}\n                activeDot={{ r: 6 }}\n              />\n            )}\n\n            {activeMetric === 'customers' && (\n              <Bar\n                yAxisId=\"customers\"\n                dataKey=\"customers\"\n                name=\"客数\"\n                fill={CHART_COLORS.customers}\n                radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}\n                maxBarSize={48}\n              />\n            )}\n\n            {/* 客単価グラフ */}\n            {activeMetric === 'averageSpend' && (\n              <Line\n                yAxisId=\"spend\"\n                type=\"monotone\"\n                dataKey=\"averageSpend\"\n                name=\"客単価\"\n                stroke={CHART_COLORS.averageSpend}\n                strokeWidth={3}\n                dot={{ r: 4, fill: CHART_COLORS.averageSpend, strokeWidth: 2, stroke: '#ffffff' }}\n                activeDot={{ r: 6 }}\n              />\n            )}\n          </ComposedChart>\n        </ResponsiveContainer>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default ChartSection;","language":"tsx"}

event: file-complete
data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/App.tsx","content":"import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';\nimport { PeriodFilter, MetricType, KpiSummary, TotalSummary } from './types';\nimport {\n  monthlySalesData,\n  filterDataByPeriod,\n  formatCurrency,\n  formatCount,\n} from './mockData';\nimport KpiCard from './components/KpiCard';\nimport ChartSection from './components/ChartSection';\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [period, setPeriod] = useState<PeriodFilter>('all');\n  const [activeMetric, setActiveMetric] = useState<MetricType>('all');\n\n  const filteredData = useMemo(() => {\n    return filterDataByPeriod(monthlySalesData, period);\n  }, [period]);\n\n  const kpiSummaries = useMemo<KpiSummary[]>(() => {\n    if (filteredData.length === 0) return [];\n\n    const currentMonth = filteredData[filteredData.length - 1];\n    const prevMonth =\n      filteredData.length > 1\n        ? filteredData[filteredData.length - 2]\n        : currentMonth;\n\n    const calcDiff = (curr: number, prev: number) => {\n      if (prev === 0) return { diff: 0, isIncrease: true };\n      const change = ((curr - prev) / prev) * 100;\n      return {\n        diff: Math.abs(change),\n        isIncrease: change >= 0,\n      };\n    };\n\n    const revDiff = calcDiff(currentMonth.revenue, prevMonth.revenue);\n    const custDiff = calcDiff(currentMonth.customers, prevMonth.customers);\n    const spendDiff = calcDiff(currentMonth.averageSpend, prevMonth.averageSpend);\n\n    return [\n      {\n        id: 'kpi-revenue',\n        title: '直近月 売上',\n        value: `¥${formatCurrency(currentMonth.revenue)}`,\n        diff: revDiff.diff,\n        isIncrease: revDiff.isIncrease,\n        unit: '円',\n      },\n      {\n        id: 'kpi-customers',\n        title: '直近月 客数',\n        value: formatCount(currentMonth.customers),\n        diff: custDiff.diff,\n        isIncrease: custDiff.isIncrease,\n        unit: '人',\n      },\n      {\n        id: 'kpi-averageSpend',\n        title: '直近月 平均客単価',\n        value: `¥${formatCurrency(currentMonth.averageSpend)}`,\n        diff: spendDiff.diff,\n        isIncrease: spendDiff.isIncrease,\n        unit: '円',\n      },\n    ];\n  }, [filteredData]);\n\n  const totals = useMemo<TotalSummary>(() => {\n    const totalRev = filteredData.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + cur.revenue, 0);\n    const totalCust = filteredData.reduce((acc, cur) => acc + cur.customers, 0);\n    const avgSpend = totalCust > 0 ? Math.round(totalRev / totalCust) : 0;\n    return {\n      revenue: totalRev,\n      customers: totalCust,\n      averageSpend: avgSpend,\n    };\n  }, [filteredData]);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"dashboard-container\">\n      {/* ヘッダーエリア */}\n      <header className=\"dashboard-header\">\n        <div className=\"header-title-group\">\n          <h1 className=\"dashboard-title\">売上分析ダッシュボード</h1>\n          <p className=\"dashboard-subtitle\">\n            月次売上・客数・客単価の統合パフォーマンス管理\n          </p>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"filter-group\" role=\"toolbar\" aria-label=\"集計期間切り替え\">\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className={`filter-btn ${period === 'all' ? 'filter-btn-active' : ''}`}\n            aria-pressed={period === 'all'}\n            onClick={() => setPeriod('all')}\n          >\n            全期間（12ヶ月）\n          </button>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className={`filter-btn ${period === '6m' ? 'filter-btn-active' : ''}`}\n            aria-pressed={period === '6m'}\n            onClick={() => setPeriod('6m')}\n          >\n            直近6ヶ月\n          </button>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className={`filter-btn ${period === '3m' ? 'filter-btn-active' : ''}`}\n            aria-pressed={period === '3m'}\n            onClick={() => setPeriod('3m')}\n          >\n            直近3ヶ月\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </header>\n\n      {/* サマリーバナー */}\n      <div className=\"summary-banner\">\n        <div className=\"summary-item\">\n          <span className=\"summary-item-label\">選択期間 合計売上</span>\n          <div className=\"summary-item-value\">\n            ¥{formatCurrency(totals.revenue)}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"summary-item\">\n          <span className=\"summary-item-label\">延べ来客数</span>\n          <div className=\"summary-item-value\">\n            {formatCount(totals.customers)} 人\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"summary-item\">\n          <span className=\"summary-item-label\">期間通算 客単価</span>\n          <div className=\"summary-item-value\">\n            ¥{formatCurrency(totals.averageSpend)}\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* KPIカード一覧 */}\n      <div className=\"kpi-grid\">\n        {kpiSummaries.map((summary) => (\n          <KpiCard key={summary.id} summary={summary} />\n        ))}\n      </div>\n\n      {/* グラフ推移セクション */}\n      <div className=\"charts-container\">\n        <ChartSection\n          data={filteredData}\n          activeMetric={activeMetric}\n          onMetricChange={setActiveMetric}\n        />\n      </div>\n\n      {/* 詳細データテーブル */}\n      <div className=\"table-container\">\n        <table className=\"data-table\">\n          <thead>\n            <tr>\n              <th className=\"text-center\">月度</th>\n              <th className=\"text-right\">売上高</th>\n              <th className=\"text-right\">客数</th>\n              <th className=\"text-right\">平均客単価</th>\n              <th className=\"text-right\">前月比（売上）</th>\n            </tr>\n          </thead>\n          <tbody>\n            {filteredData.map((row, index) => {\n              const prev = index > 0 ?
